def require(v,m):
 if not bool(v):raise ValueError(m)
def literal(a,b):
 import torch
 if torch.is_tensor(a):return torch.is_tensor(b) and a.dtype==b.dtype and a.shape==b.shape and torch.equal(a.cpu(),b.cpu())
 if isinstance(a,dict):return isinstance(b,dict) and set(a)==set(b) and all(literal(a[k],b[k]) for k in a)
 if isinstance(a,(tuple,list)):return type(a) is type(b) and len(a)==len(b) and all(literal(x,y) for x,y in zip(a,b))
 return type(a) is type(b) and a==b
def json_value(x):
 import torch
 if torch.is_tensor(x):return x.detach().cpu().tolist()
 if isinstance(x,dict):return {k:json_value(v) for k,v in x.items()}
 if isinstance(x,(tuple,list)):return [json_value(v) for v in x]
 return x
def termination_cache_contract(source_path):

 require(sha=='cb6333f11fa6472f7d8034bae9835714e83ae3edde97894b767f1351a7eea99d','Actual original cached termination source pin')

 require(len(ret)==1 and ast.dump(ret[0])==ast.dump(ast.parse('self._term_dones[:, self._term_name_to_term_idx[name]]',mode='eval').body),'Cached get_term semantics')
 return dict(available=True,source_path=str(source_path),source_SHA=sha,names_attribute='_term_names',values_attribute='_term_dones',actual_get_term_cached_no_recompute=True)
class Stream:
 def __init__(self,p):self.path=Path(p);self.file=self.path.open('xb');self.hash=hashlib.sha256();self.rows=0;self.bytes=0
 def write(self,v):
  b=(json.dumps(json_value(v),allow_nan=False,separators=(',',':'))+'\n').encode();self.file.write(b);self.hash.update(b);self.rows+=1;self.bytes+=len(b)
 def close(self):
  self.file.flush();self.file.close();require(self.path.stat().st_size==self.bytes,'Producer byte count')
  return dict(path=str(self.path),bytes=self.bytes,SHA=self.hash.hexdigest(),rows=self.rows,producer_single_write_hash=True,no_content_reread=True)

Use the exact ROOT170 `OWN_MATCHED_ROLLING_INLINE_PARAMETERS_V6.json`,33226B/SHA6a0a425c065ff78fe0e48e220b62f711125261692d3b5517756c4998d2b48a33. The API remains compatible with ROOT's cached observer and adds no SDK, file, device, model or random operation. The new gain/tau0/reference replace old166 values; constructor provenance checks the matched168 source. ROOT171 owns actual plant/resource/Task admission. This is a ground foundation, not a fulljump teacher.

The new necessary LOCAL CPU purpose completed once with no error and .125s math time. It read the170 gain/parameters, the newly extracted169 selected0..4 subset and frozen old parameter source once each. It compiled the frozen pure old controller only to compare four newly exported real inputs. It did not rerun old V3/166 tests, read original167 raw/oldsmall, recompute168, execute MATLAB/SDK/GPU/remote, or read a model. The original169 derived report was read once by a separately frozen extraction purpose; only `new167.selected` was retained. All saved reports, the preparation-command error and source files are pending ROOT ordinary Git append after the165 cutoff.

Correct timing matters: decision k consumes selected[k-1].terminal_state, whereas selected[k].teacher is its output. The four old decisions were reconstructed within2.66e-14 across raw, virtual coordinates/rates, error and nominal torque. Own FK body origins matched all cached31 body positions within2.41e-7m in these samples, a same-source float32 diagnostic, not a new numerical or physical gate. Original physical RPY supplied orientation; the historical quaternion label and body quaternion ordering were not guessed.

The source proves action-term processed queue order through `LEG_ORDER` and `preserve_order=True`, the delay adapter's unchanged processed-actions submission, and observer concatenation of legs/wheels. The sampled terminal applied-target vector agrees with each old named processed target to4.07e-7. This says nothing about actuator internal vector order or actual substep torque, which remains UNKNOWN.

The real first input differs materially from the previous zero-speed constructed cold example. After bootstrap it has pitch .005307rad, left hip q .329850rad and dq -.540348rad/s. Old decision1 requests wheel[-3.09454,-3.05570]Nm, not the old constructed[-6.31391,-6.31561]. That correction is retained. Applying the new170 gain to those same saved inputs is **counterfactual**, not actual new closed-loop motion. Its first requested torque is[-7.30837,10.29564,-8.07093,10.98609,-5.45266,-5.41571]Nm; all four counterfactual decisions avoid preinverse clipping. New wheel effort is close to the original5.77591Nm bound. No gain or weight was adjusted to obtain this result.

The one new400-control/1600-callback nominal linear test starts from the actual first-input virtual ERROR, not a written physical state. It queues leg targets2callbacks/wheel0, computes PD from current q/dq at each2.5ms callback, then holds clipped torque during that callback's linear integration. Preinverse/raw/CAD and post-PD signed clip counts/fractions are all zero for this constructed model run. Maximum after-PD nominal leg effort is12.0054Nm and wheel5.45266Nm. Peak backward displacement is5.575cm; after4s forward position error is .419mm and velocity -.000559m/s. q-domain flags are diagnostic only and zero here. This does not simulate original mesh/facet contact, slip, delayDR, nonlinear body motion or impact, and is not a PhysX stability PASS.

The new callback-start-PD linear map has radius .984146846. ROOT170's retained .983960656 map exponentiates continuous PD inside each callback; the difference in assumptions is now explicit. Neither map certifies actual original delayDR or physical stability. The original170 gain and report were not altered or recomputed. Python and NumPy random states were unchanged.

Bootstrap holds actual q for one contact-unknown control. Subsequent balance uses readonly first cached position/heading with matched virtual targets. Actual air requests a diagnostic stop before further ground action. Existing physical guards remain authoritative; no submicrometre, q-hard-stop or whole-case closure rule was added. Unexpected physical behavior is to be preserved and diagnosed in a new namespace.

Latest qualified neural phase is165, not134; main165 is closed and independent. ROOT171 is a separate4s/model-free ground foundation under fresh resource authority. The final backup still needs acceleration, crouch/push, true continuous double-wheel flight across270mm, full-footprint landing and original top12/stable200callbacks/recovery. No fixed ground gain is labelled a complete jumping controller.

import math

ACTION_ORDER=('left_crank','left_hip','right_crank','right_hip','left_wheel_spin','right_wheel_spin')
SCHEMA='mod4.own_lqr_matched_rolling.ground_foundation_parameters.v6'
def require(v,m):
    if not v:raise ValueError(m)
def finite(v,n,label):
    require(isinstance(v,(list,tuple)) and len(v)==n and all(isinstance(x,(int,float)) and not isinstance(x,bool) and math.isfinite(x) for x in v),label)
    return tuple(float(x) for x in v)
def add(a,b):return tuple(x+y for x,y in zip(a,b))
def sub(a,b):return tuple(x-y for x,y in zip(a,b))
def mul(a,s):return tuple(x*s for x in a)
def dot(a,b):return sum(x*y for x,y in zip(a,b))
def cross(a,b):return (a[1]*b[2]-a[2]*b[1],a[2]*b[0]-a[0]*b[2],a[0]*b[1]-a[1]*b[0])
def mv(R,v):return tuple(dot(row,v) for row in R)
def transpose(R):return tuple(zip(*R))
def mm(A,B):return tuple(tuple(dot(row,col) for col in zip(*B)) for row in A)
def qmat(q):
    n=math.sqrt(dot(q,q));require(n>0,'Ownsourcejointquaternion');w,x,y,z=(v/n for v in q)
    return ((1-2*(y*y+z*z),2*(x*y-w*z),2*(x*z+w*y)),(2*(x*y+w*z),1-2*(x*x+z*z),2*(y*z-w*x)),(2*(x*z-w*y),2*(y*z+w*x),1-2*(x*x+y*y)))
def rpy_matrix(roll,pitch,yaw):
    cr,sr,cp,sp,cy,sy=math.cos(roll),math.sin(roll),math.cos(pitch),math.sin(pitch),math.cos(yaw),math.sin(yaw)
    return ((cy*cp,cy*sp*sr-sy*cr,cy*sp*cr+sy*sr),(sy*cp,sy*sp*sr+cy*cr,sy*sp*cr-cy*sr),(-sp,cp*sr,cp*cr))
def angle_error(a,b):return math.atan2(math.sin(a-b),math.cos(a-b))
def clip(x,lo,hi):return max(lo,min(hi,x))

class OwnMatchedRollingGroundFoundation:

        require(all(x>0 for x in (*self.effort,*self.stall,*self.speed)),'Positiveoriginalmotorparameters')
        require(parameters['control_dt_s']==.01 and parameters['diagnostic_controls']==400,'Explicitground400diagnostic')
        require(parameters['selected_ground_velocity_m_s']==0 and parameters['historical_public_command_m_s']==.5,'Explicitdiagnosticgoalnotjumpcommand')
        self.rows=[];bychild={r['child']:(n,r) for n,r in self.joints.items()};seen={'chassis'}
        for child in self.bodies[1:]:
            n,r=bychild[child];require(r['parent'] in seen,'Owndatatopologicalorder');seen.add(child)
            self.rows.append((n,r,self.bodies.index(r['parent']),qmat(r['local_rot0']),transpose(qmat(r['local_rot1']))))
        self.last_control=None;self.episode=None;self.anchor=None;self.anchor_yaw=None

    def _named(self,names,q,dq=None):
        names=tuple(names);q=finite(q,30,'finiteactualq30');require(len(names)==30 and len(set(names))==30 and set(names)==set(self.names),'Exactactualjointnames')
        perm=[names.index(n) for n in self.names];q=tuple(q[i] for i in perm)
        if dq is not None:dq=finite(dq,30,'finiteactualdq30');dq=tuple(dq[i] for i in perm)
        return q,dq

    def _fk(self,p,R,v,w,q,dq):
        P=[p];Rs=[R];V=[v];W=[w]
        for n,r,parent,R0,R1T in self.rows:
            j=self.names.index(n);a=q[j];ad=dq[j];A=mm(Rs[parent],R0);axis=tuple(row[0] for row in A);anchor=mv(Rs[parent],r['local_pos0'])
            if r['type']=='hinge':
                c,s=math.cos(a),math.sin(a);CR=mm(mm(A,((1.,0.,0.),(0.,c,-s),(0.,s,c))),R1T);cw=add(W[parent],mul(axis,ad));offset=mv(CR,r['local_pos1']);cp=sub(add(P[parent],anchor),offset)
                cv=sub(add(V[parent],cross(W[parent],anchor)),cross(cw,offset))
            elif r['type']=='slide':
                CR=mm(A,R1T);cw=W[parent];offset=sub(add(anchor,mul(axis,a)),mv(CR,r['local_pos1']));cp=add(P[parent],offset);cv=add(add(V[parent],cross(W[parent],offset)),mul(axis,ad))
            else:raise ValueError('Ownhinge/slideonly')
            P.append(cp);Rs.append(CR);V.append(cv);W.append(cw)
        return P,Rs,V,W

    def _virtual(self,p,R,v,w,q,dq):
        P,Rs,V,W=self._fk(p,R,v,w,q,dq);bx=tuple(row[0] for row in R);h=math.hypot(bx[0],bx[1]);require(h>1e-8,'Headingundefined')
        ex=(bx[0]/h,bx[1]/h,0.);ey=(-ex[1],ex[0],0.);bxd=cross(w,bx);yawdot=(bx[0]*bxd[1]-bx[1]*bxd[0])/(h*h);exd=mul(ey,yawdot)
        lengths=[];theta=[];ld=[];td=[]
        def anchor(joint):
            row=self.joints[joint];b=self.bodies.index(row['parent']);offset=mv(Rs[b],row['local_pos0']);return add(P[b],offset),add(V[b],cross(W[b],offset))
        for side in ('left','right'):
            hp,hv=anchor(side+'_hip');ap,av=anchor(side+'_wheel_spin');r=sub(hp,ap);rd=sub(hv,av);L=math.sqrt(dot(r,r));rx=dot(r,ex);rz=r[2];require(L>.01 and rx*rx+rz*rz>1e-12,'Nondegenerateownleg')
            lengths.append(L);theta.append(math.atan2(rx,rz));ld.append(dot(r,rd)/L);drx=dot(rd,ex)+dot(r,exd);td.append((rz*drx-rx*rd[2])/(rx*rx+rz*rz))
        return lengths,theta,ld,td,dot(w,ey)

    def _signed_clip(self,tau,velocity):
        out=[]
        for t,v,e,s,no in zip(tau,velocity,self.effort,self.stall,self.speed):
            vv=clip(v,-no*(1+e/s),no*(1+e/s));hi=min(s*(1-vv/no),e);lo=max(s*(-1-vv/no),-e);out.append(clip(t,lo,hi))
        return tuple(out)

    def _raw_from_torque(self,q,dq,requested):
        velocity=tuple(dq[i] for i in self.ids);bounded=self._signed_clip(requested,velocity)
        target=tuple(q[self.ids[j]]+(bounded[j]+2*dq[self.ids[j]])/60 for j in range(4))
        wheels=tuple(velocity[4+j]+bounded[4+j]/.6 for j in range(2));raw_before=tuple(x/.5 for x in target)+tuple(x/10 for x in wheels)
        raw=tuple(clip(x,-6,6) for x in raw_before);processed=list(tuple(.5*x for x in raw[:4])+tuple(10*x for x in raw[4:]))
        for j in (0,2):processed[j]=clip(processed[j],processed[j+1]-1.0843,processed[j+1]+.85)
        # Send the already-CAD-projected targets throughoriginalrawcodec.
        final=tuple(processed[j]/(.5 if j<4 else 10.) for j in range(6))
        nominal=tuple(60*(processed[j]-q[self.ids[j]])-2*dq[self.ids[j]] for j in range(4))+tuple(.6*(processed[j]-velocity[j]) for j in (4,5))
        nominal=self._signed_clip(self._signed_clip(nominal,velocity),velocity)
        return final,dict(requested_nominal_torque6=list(requested),bounded_nominal_torque6=list(bounded),nominal_after_PD_CAD_two_clips6=list(nominal),
            raw_before_limits6=list(raw_before),raw_saturation6=[abs(a-b)>1e-10 for a,b in zip(raw_before,raw)],
            nominal_torque_limit_active6=[abs(a-b)>1e-10 for a,b in zip(requested,bounded)],
            CAD_projection_active2=[abs(processed[j]-.5*raw[j])>1e-10 for j in (0,2)],
            processed_target6=processed,nominal_not_measured_effort=True)

    def step(self,observation):
        kind=observation['kind']
        if kind=='initial_contact_unknown':
            require(self.last_control is None,'Bootstraponlyonce');q,_=self._named(observation['joint_names'],observation['q30']);self.last_control=0
            raw=tuple(clip(q[i]/.5,-6,6) for i in self.ids[:4])+(0.,0.)
            return dict(raw6=list(raw),phase='BOOTSTRAP_ACTUAL_Q_HOLD_CONTACT_UNKNOWN',global_control=0,successful_skill=False,ground_foundation_only=True)
        require(kind=='original_cached_physical','Originalcachedcallbackonly');o=observation['source_observation']
        control=o['global_control'];require(type(control) is int and self.last_control is not None and control==self.last_control+1,'Exactnext-actionclock')
        done=o.get('original_done',o.get('actual_done'));require(type(done) is bool and not done,'Noactionafteractualoriginaldone')
        episode=o['episode_id'];require(type(episode) is int,'Actualepisodeid');require(self.episode is None or episode==self.episode,'Actualepisodechanged, ROOTmustbootstrapnewcase')
        names=o.get('named_joint_order',o.get('joint_names'));q,dq=self._named(names,o['q30'],o['dq30'])
        p=finite(o['actual_root_xyz'],3,'Actualcachedrootxyz');v=finite(o['world_velocity3'],3,'Actualcachedworldv');w=finite(o['world_angular_velocity3'],3,'Actualcachedworldomega')
        roll,pitch,yaw=(float(o[k]) for k in ('root_roll','root_pitch','root_yaw'));require(all(math.isfinite(x) for x in (roll,pitch,yaw)),'PhysicalRPYfinite')
        air=o['clean_air_now'];require(type(air) is bool,'Originalairboolnotfakezero')
        if self.anchor is None:self.anchor=p;self.anchor_yaw=yaw;self.episode=episode
        self.last_control=control
        if air:
            raw=tuple(clip(q[i]/.5,-6,6) for i in self.ids[:4])+(clip(dq[self.ids[4]]/10,-6,6),clip(dq[self.ids[5]]/10,-6,6))
            return dict(raw6=list(raw),phase='ACTUAL_AIR_OUTSIDE_GROUND_FOUNDATION',diagnostic_stop_requested=True,
                stop_reason='Genuineairneedsseparatestagedbackupcontroller; notfixedgaingroundmodel',successful_skill=False,ground_foundation_only=True)
        R=rpy_matrix(roll,pitch,yaw);L,theta,Ld,thetad,phid=self._virtual(p,R,v,w,q,dq);ex=(math.cos(self.anchor_yaw),math.sin(self.anchor_yaw),0.)
        actual=(dot(sub(p,self.anchor),ex),pitch,L[0],theta[0],L[1],theta[1]);rates=(dot(v,ex),phid,Ld[0],thetad[0],Ld[1],thetad[1])
        error=list(a-b for a,b in zip(actual,self.reference));error[1]=angle_error(actual[1],self.reference[1]);error[3]=angle_error(actual[3],self.reference[3]);error[5]=angle_error(actual[5],self.reference[5]);error=tuple(error)+tuple(rates)
        requested=tuple(t-dot(k,error) for t,k in zip(self.tau0,self.K));raw,details=self._raw_from_torque(q,dq,requested)
        require(all(math.isfinite(x) and abs(x)<=6.0000001 for x in raw),'Originalfiniteaction6bounds')
        return dict(raw6=list(raw),phase='OWN_MATCHED_ROLLING_LQR_ZERO_GROUND_HOLD_FOUNDATION',global_control=control,episode_id=episode,
            state_error12=list(error),measured_virtual_y6=list(actual),measured_virtual_velocity6=list(rates),target_virtual_y6=list(self.reference),
            readonly_initial_position_anchor3=list(self.anchor),actual_root_rpy_source='originalphysicalcallback, notroot13quatlabel',
            raw_saturation6=details['raw_saturation6'],requested_nominal_torque6=details['requested_nominal_torque6'],nominal_details=details,
            actual_air=air,actual_prepared=o.get('prepared_now'),actual_public_request=o.get('request_active'),
            selected_diagnostic_goal='zero-groundhold; originalpublic0p5commandunchanged',yaw_full_feedback_not_claimed=True,
            actual_qdomain_diagnostic_only=[self.names[j] for j,n in enumerate(self.names) if self.joints[n].get('limits_si') and not self.joints[n]['limits_si'][0]<=q[j]<=self.joints[n]['limits_si'][1]],
            qdomain_diagnostic_not_added_physical_termination=True,
            ground_foundation_only=True,successful_skill=False,full270backup_requires_separate_accel_push_air_landing_validation=True)


def build_controller(parameters):
    return OwnMatchedRollingGroundFoundation(parameters)

def zoh(A,B,dt):

  ROOT170_continuous_intra_callback_PD_spectral_radius=G['spectral_radius_nominal_leg2callback_wheel0_target_delay'],

# Latest165 actual / imagination / reference display sources

These sources have not been executed by the author. No checkpoint, raw control data, NPZ, actual large reference, mesh/media, MATLAB, SDK, GPU or remote content was loaded by the author. ROOT runs the new CPU purpose after the new172 case has really returned and transferred. All old132/165/V6B sources and cases remain unchanged.172 is separate from the pending171 foundation.

`source/prepare172_latest165_gray_blue_orange_CPU_once_v1.py --purpose ROOT_FROZEN_FILLED_PURPOSE.json --out NEW_PREPARED_DIRECTORY` is a complete CPU-only adapter from the old132 display flow. It uses current165 source modules and the matching165 checkpoint Pi+WM. ROOT must fill and freeze the template with actual producer paths/bytes/SHA, actual joint and SDK encoder names, source pins and validation tolerances. The template is intentionally not executable as supplied. The checkpoint is one integrity pass, seek and one decode; controls and reference each have one content read/parse. Critic/optimizer are not constructed and all model/normalizer tensors are checked unchanged. Errors and counts remain in the unique consumed output.

Current165 differs from old132 in three material ways. Its goal is the own MATLAB162 table through `OwnMatlabTrackingReference` and `OwnMatlabTrackingMdp`, not the old static Fudan endpoint. Its `flat91` function reexports the configured fixed SI root and own midpoint/half-range leg conditioning; configuration is required before Pi forward. Actual SDK encoder order is an explicit input distinct from raw action order. The source follows the exact current learner order: action, WM step,17bit `mdp.advance_reward_frame`, predicted observation/history and next goal. No future actual action/state/observation/process enters a branch.

The actual ten observations are reconstructed exactly from saved86-dimensional inputs and the known fresh-episode first-observation-repeat/shift rule, confirmed in new172 driver source. Each branch has the actual127-state/privateframe/staticxi31 at its start. The blue policy runs closed loop for32 steps and records predicted process, softdone and survival at every step. The HUD shows termination probability and cumulative survival, marks PRED_STOP at or after any step reaching .99, and labels the continued raw rollout as unsuitable for a viable plan or physical future after that stop. It does not use the orange table as transition truth. Future known goals are the original native ten-goal conditioning, not future actual observations. Blue past publicage2000 is explicitly marked invalid, without resetting its state or fabricating physical motion.

Gray uses only new172 recorded PhysX31-body endpoint poses. ROOT reports that this case returned1046 controls/4184 original callbacks/10.46s with closure as its sole original termination. The preparer accepts any completed original case length up to2000, does not require20s or skill success, and does not replay physics. Its reported height peak is100Hz control endpoints, never a full400Hz peak or population rate. The runtime schema is checked from the new source; the author has not read that actual report or control file.

Orange body poses use the same reference table's p/wxyz/q positions through ownFK at publicage, with velocities zeroed for position-only drawing. Wheelspin/guide values with mask0 remain explicitly drawing placeholders. Orange is a mathematical goal path, not observed physical data or a validated successful teacher. Original mask/process-goal conventions remain unchanged. Gray time and orange time match the terminal reward frame's decoded publicage; the blue branch inputtime and future targettime are separately shown, so a future blue endpoint is not mistaken for a simultaneous measured robot.

`source/view172_latest165_true_MOD4_gray_blue_orange_v1.py --prepared PREPARED_DIRECTORY --out NEW_VIEWER_DIRECTORY` opens a visible native MuJoCo/GLFW renderer. It loads the prepared pose artifact only and uses277 existing true baked own visual meshes across three layers:93 mocap bodies/831 robot geoms. No collision-primitive substitute, pose rescaling, neural inference or `mj_step` occurs. Default wide view includes the roughly-1.5m backoff and .75m platform. Each viewer run has a new output directory preserving its original errors/framebuffer/identity.

The default timeline is the observed actual case. Keys B/O show blue/orange, H changes8/16/32-step horizon, Space pauses, R replays the same recording,1/2/3 change playback speed. T can show the complete20s reference; after the actual recording ends, gray and blue are hidden and the HUD says ended. This avoids inventing physical motion after the original closure termination. The rendered initial framebuffer and window start/exit receipts are written for ROOT's native visual review.

ROOT should inspect/compile these source files before consuming the new CPU model/raw purpose. Actual execution, numeric/first-action results, visible window and screenshots are still pending ROOT. All saved source/reader errors and later actual reports require ordinary Git archival; no archive claim is made here.

"""ROOT-run NEW172 CPU prepare. Author SOURCE ONLY; never run old132 once.
Gray recorded165 PhysX / blue same165 Pi+WM / orange own162 goal reference.
"""
import os
os.environ['CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES']=''
from pathlib import Path
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import sys
import time
import traceback

COUNTS={'controls_reads':0,'reference_reads':0,'checkpoint_integrity_passes':0,'checkpoint_decodes':0}
READ_RECEIPTS=[]
def require(v,m):
 if not bool(v):raise ValueError(m)
def H(p):
 with Path(p).open('rb') as f:return hashlib.file_digest(f,'sha256').hexdigest()
def put(p,v):
 with Path(p).open('x',encoding='utf-8') as f:json.dump(v,f,indent=2,allow_nan=False)
def pinjson(row,kind=None):
 p=Path(row['path']);st=p.stat();require(st.st_size==row['bytes'],'Pinned bytes '+str(p));b=p.read_bytes()
 if kind is not None:COUNTS[kind]+=1
 actual=hashlib.sha256(b).hexdigest();READ_RECEIPTS.append(dict(path=str(p),bytes=len(b),sha256=actual,byte_reads=1,JSON_parses_attempted=1))
 require(actual==row['sha256'],'Pinned SHA '+str(p));require(p.stat().st_mtime_ns==st.st_mtime_ns,'Input changed '+str(p))
 return json.loads(b.decode('utf-8-sig'))
def at(value,path):
 for key in path:value=value[key]
 return value
def scalar(x):
 if isinstance(x,list):require(len(x)==1,'One-element scalar');return scalar(x[0])
 require(type(x) is int,'Exact integer clock');return x
def artifact(p,kind):
 p=Path(p);return dict(path=str(p),bytes=p.stat().st_size,sha256=H(p),typed_kind=kind)

def main(purposePath,outDir):
 started=time.monotonic();out=Path(outDir);require(not out.exists(),'NEW output only; no once replay');out.mkdir(parents=True)
 q=json.loads(Path(purposePath).read_bytes());require(q['schema']=='mod4.visual172.latest165.cpu_purpose.v1' and q['latest_qualified_phase']==165,'Latest165 typed purpose')
 put(out/'PURPOSE172_CONSUMED.json',dict(purpose=q,source_sha256=H(__file__),started_epoch=time.time(),author_source_only_no_actual_run_claim=True))
 planned=dict(poses=dict(path=str(out/'ACTUAL165_BLUE165_ORANGE162_POSES172.npz'),typed_kind='display_only_three_producer_roles_not_training_truth'),
  shapes=dict(path=str(out/'DISPLAY172_ACTUAL_SHAPES.json'),typed_kind='recorded_original_geometry'),
  roles=dict(gray='actual172 originalPhysX165',blue='same165 Pi+WM closedloopH32 prediction',orange='ownMATLAB162 goal positions not validatedteacher'),bytes_and_SHA='Pending writer close, published only after completion')
 put(out/'ARTIFACTS_PLANNED_FROM_START.json',planned)
 import numpy as np
 import torch
 import psutil

def H(p):
 with Path(p).open('rb') as f:return hashlib.file_digest(f,'sha256').hexdigest()
def require(v,m):
 if not bool(v):raise ValueError(m)
def pin(row):

def fmt(x):return ' '.join(f'{float(v):.10g}' for v in np.asarray(x).ravel())
def make_scene(render,visual,out,origin):

 def show(layer,p,R,visible=True):

  for i,mid in enumerate(ids[layer]):mujoco.mju_mat2Quat(data.mocap_quat[mid],np.asarray(R[i],dtype=np.float64).ravel())
 def key(w,k,scan,action,mods):
  if action not in (glfw.PRESS,glfw.REPEAT):return
  if k==glfw.KEY_ESCAPE:glfw.set_window_should_close(w,True)
  elif k==glfw.KEY_SPACE:state['paused']=not state['paused']
  elif k==glfw.KEY_R:state['clock']=0.
  elif k==glfw.KEY_B:state['blue']=not state['blue']
  elif k==glfw.KEY_O:state['orange']=not state['orange']
  elif k==glfw.KEY_T:state['reference_timeline']=not state['reference_timeline'];state['clock']=0.
  elif k==glfw.KEY_H:state['horizon']={8:16,16:32,32:8}[state['horizon']]
  elif k in (glfw.KEY_1,glfw.KEY_2,glfw.KEY_3):state['speed']={glfw.KEY_1:1.,glfw.KEY_2:.25,glfw.KEY_3:.1}[k]
  elif k==glfw.KEY_LEFT:state['clock']=max(0.,state['clock']-.1)
  elif k==glfw.KEY_RIGHT:state['clock']=min((20. if state['reference_timeline'] else duration)-.01,state['clock']+.1)
 def cursor(w,x,y):
  lx,ly=state['cursor'];state['cursor']=(x,y);_,height=glfw.get_window_size(w)
  if glfw.get_mouse_button(w,glfw.MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT)==glfw.PRESS:mujoco.mjv_moveCamera(model,mujoco.mjtMouse.mjMOUSE_ROTATE_V,(x-lx)/height,(y-ly)/height,cam)

# Reason-only review of already-generated172 poses

The complete NumPy entry is `source/diagnose_saved172_softstop_reasons_once_v173.py --purpose ROOT_FILLED_PINNED_PURPOSE.json --out NEW173_DIRECTORY`. It is source only: the author has not executed a probe, parsed an NPZ/proof/report, read a model/raw/reference or touched the visible172 window. ROOT must freeze/admit the new purpose, run its normal fresh local process check, inspect/compile the source, and retain the output/error. No old once, model or case is replayed.

Only ten existing NPZ members are needed. Actual/blue body31 positions and rotations provide the six anchor distances and chassis Rzz. Actual public ages and branch start ages give control endpoint times and blue step ages. Stored blue soft_done, survival and valid masks are compared with reconstruction; origin is retained for coordinate provenance. The other members, including orange, actions, wheel geometry and process codes, are not decoded. One readonly NPZ handle performs integrity verification then a single load; each requested array is read once. The small prepared producer proof and source C/G are each read once; selected source files are SHA-checked, never imported. No model, controlJSONL, extra raw or large reference read is needed.

Current165 `OwnMatlabTrackingMdp` inherits the `OwnFudanStepMdp.soft_termination` override, not the ancestor's old .12m height sigmoid. It computes a32-control engineering tilt risk from `(cos(.75)-Rzz)/.04`, converted to per-step hazard. Rzz below cos(.75) or publicage at least2000 sets certain termination. The added closure risk is `clamp(closure/.002,0,1)^2`, active from age10 and similarly converted to32-step hazard; closure strictly greater than2mm from age10 sets certain termination. No q, height, contact, arena or mimic input occurs in this selected head.

Without a hard mask, each capped horizon hazard has finite maximum and their combined per-step value is at most about .578, below .99. Thus stored head values at least .99 should correspond to hard closure, hard tilt or hard timeout under the admitted source. The parser reports nonexclusive reasons, first-stop combinations, all six anchor errors and the root Rzz/publicage at the first recorded stop of each branch. It also retains soft-value/survival differences and float32/float64 threshold disagreements. It never changes a reason merely to match the recorded28/80 number.

Small analytic positive/negative examples in the same parser cover upright/closed, closure before age10, exact2mm versus above2mm, tilt threshold versus below, and age1999 versus2000. They are scalar source-math checks, not a physical robot probe, full oldfixture rerun or training gate. Float32 pose/anchor operation order can differ from the original Torch/GPU computation around strict thresholds. Those differences are findings, not failures of the original physical guard and not a new numerical requirement.

The missing q/dq prevent reliable joint/gas-stroke domain, mimic or motor PD/envelope diagnosis. Missing contacts/aux/queue prevent unloaded/support, actual delayed target/effort, sixcue process replay or genuine flight/fullfootprint/top12/stable200 qualification. These quantities are not covertly added to the selected soft head. The NPZ is sufficient for this analytic-head reason decomposition but not for those broader questions.

Gray anchor/tilt values are reported only as100Hz endpoint context, with the saved original termination receipt preserved. Actual172 terminated by original closure at10.46s; a reconstructed gray endpoint is not the complete400Hz physical callback trace. A blue branch follows imagined observations and actions, so its future action sequence is not generally the recorded gray sequence. A STOP reason inside this head therefore does not establish a true or false physical forecast, isolated WM accuracy or the sole cause of failed jumping.

The new171 original-plant4s ground foundation is separate and needs no oldfixture rerun here; full270 jump backup remains future work. Original172 artifacts, viewerV1 missing-psutil error and ROOT's separate visibleV2 remain unchanged. All source and eventual reason reports are pending ROOT Git archival; no archive claim is made by the author.

"""ROOT-run NEW reason-only NumPy purpose; never loads a neural model/raw.
Reconstruct the selected165 analytic head from the saved body31 visual poses.

import os
os.environ['CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES']=''
from pathlib import Path
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import math
import time
import traceback
import numpy as np

MEMBERS=('actual_position','actual_rotation','actual_public_age','blue_position','blue_rotation','blue_soft_done','blue_survival','blue_valid','branch_input_public_age','origin')
SOFT_SOURCE={
 'native_fudan_step_adapter_candidate_v2.py':'147b27b5596616576531a89bb1700369d08099f23985aa4a91ef38b53fdd46bc',
 'jump_process_command_reward122_v1.py':'ca6fc1d6d9eefe5738e10eb2e89b9c34502ae731905a75b7713c7f059e801780',
 'matlab144_trajectory_tracking_v2.py':'e1ae8c0a342c484dac63c3015436d05623f8d8d28ba9f2bed75bf867665aef20',
 'private_phase_frame_v1.py':'79ab8e95dff4e51b41dcfe38e36223f7a0e6501286d33ad37ca7cf65d486b5d9'}

def require(v,m):
 if not bool(v):raise ValueError(m)
def sha(b):return hashlib.sha256(b).hexdigest()
def save(p,v):
 with Path(p).open('x',encoding='utf-8') as f:json.dump(v,f,indent=2,allow_nan=False)
def pin_json(row,ledger,key):
 p=Path(row['path']);st=p.stat();require(st.st_size==row['bytes'],'Input bytes '+key);b=p.read_bytes();s=sha(b)
 ledger.append(dict(key=key,path=str(p),bytes=len(b),sha256=s,byte_reads=1,JSON_parses_attempted=1));require(s==row['sha256'],'Input SHA '+key);require(p.stat().st_mtime_ns==st.st_mtime_ns,'Input changed '+key)
 return json.loads(b.decode('utf-8-sig'))

def closure(p,R,C,body_names,dtype):
 p=np.asarray(p,dtype=dtype);R=np.asarray(R,dtype=dtype);ids={n:i for i,n in enumerate(body_names)};values=[];names=[]
 for name,row in C['closures'].items():
  i,j=ids[row['body0']],ids[row['body1']];a=np.asarray(row['anchor0_m'],dtype=dtype);b=np.asarray(row['anchor1_m'],dtype=dtype)
  x=p[...,i,:]+np.einsum('...ij,j->...i',R[...,i,:,:],a);y=p[...,j,:]+np.einsum('...ij,j->...i',R[...,j,:,:],b)
  values.append(np.sqrt(np.sum(np.square(x-y),axis=-1)));names.append(name)
 error=np.stack(values,axis=-1);return error,error.max(-1),names

def head_terms(closure_m,rzz,ages,dtype):
 c=np.asarray(closure_m,dtype=dtype);rzz=np.asarray(rzz,dtype=dtype);age=np.asarray(ages,dtype=np.int64);one=np.asarray(1.,dtype=dtype)
 z=(np.asarray(math.cos(.75),dtype=dtype)-rzz)/np.asarray(.04,dtype=dtype)
 # Stable sigmoid, same mathematical function as selected Torch source.
 ez=np.exp(-np.abs(z));horizon=np.where(z>=0,one/(one+ez),ez/(one+ez))
 cap=np.asarray(1.-1e-6,dtype=dtype);th=-np.expm1(np.log1p(-np.minimum(horizon,cap))/np.asarray(32.,dtype=dtype))
 tilt=rzz<np.asarray(math.cos(.75),dtype=dtype);timeout=age>=2000;original=np.where(tilt|timeout,one,th)
 risk=np.square(np.clip(c/np.asarray(.002,dtype=dtype),np.asarray(0.,dtype=dtype),one));risk=np.where(age>=10,risk,np.asarray(0.,dtype=dtype))
 ch=-np.expm1(np.log1p(-np.minimum(risk,cap))/np.asarray(32.,dtype=dtype));certain=(age>=10)&(c>np.asarray(.002,dtype=dtype))
 combined=one-(one-original)*(one-ch);total=np.where(certain,one,combined)
 return dict(total=total,tilt_horizon_risk=horizon,tilt_perstep_hazard=th,closure_horizon_risk=risk,closure_perstep_hazard=ch,
  hard_tilt=tilt,hard_timeout=timeout,hard_closure=certain)

def labels(parts,index):return [n for n in ('hard_closure','hard_tilt','hard_timeout') if bool(parts[n][index])]
def fixtures():
 examples=[('upright_closed',0.,1.,20,False),('closure_ignored_before_age10',.01,1.,9,False),
  ('closure_exact2mm_no_strict_hard',.002,1.,10,False),('closure_above2mm',.00201,1.,10,True),
  ('tilt_at_exact_cos_threshold',0.,math.cos(.75),20,False),('tilt_below_cos_threshold',0.,math.cos(.75)-.001,20,True),
  ('age1999',0.,1.,1999,False),('age2000',0.,1.,2000,True)]
 rows=[]
 for name,c,r,a,expected in examples:
  h=head_terms(np.array([c]),np.array([r]),np.array([a]),np.float32);got=bool(h['total'][0]>=.99)
  rows.append(dict(name=name,closure_m=c,Rzz=r,public_age=a,head=float(h['total'][0]),hard_reasons=labels(h,0),expected_stop=expected,actual_stop=got,matched=got==expected))
 upper=1.-(1e-6)**(2./32.)
 return dict(source_math_only=rows,all_categories_matched=all(r['matched'] for r in rows),
  maximum_combined_without_hard_mask_real_arithmetic=upper,reason_p_ge_0p99_requires_a_hard_mask=upper<.99,
  no_q_contact_or_height_argument_in_selected_head=True,not_a_physical_robot_probe=True)

def run(q,R):
 start=time.monotonic();proof=pin_json(q['prepared_proof'],R['inputs'],'172_prepared_proof');C=pin_json(q['import_contract'],R['inputs'],'own_anchor_contract');G=pin_json(q['own_geometry'],R['inputs'],'own_explicit_body_order')
 require(proof['schema']=='mod4.visual172.prepared165three_roles.v1' and proof['passed'] and proof['blue_closed_loop_same165'] and proof['models_norms_literal_unchanged'],'Actual172 completed producer roles')
 require(C['source_mjcf_sha256']=='fbb0393883d31ac96f8cd58a184585470222f7daf96af6aa5d0748a1752c366c','Own anchor identity')
 require(len(C['closures'])==6 and len(G['body_names'])==31,'Own6anchors/body31')
 rows={r['relative']:r for r in q['soft_source_rows']};require(set(SOFT_SOURCE)<=set(rows),'Selected head source pins')
 for name,expected in SOFT_SOURCE.items():
  row=rows[name];b=Path(row['path']).read_bytes();s=sha(b);R['inputs'].append(dict(key='selected_head_source:'+name,path=row['path'],bytes=len(b),sha256=s,byte_reads=1));require(s==row['sha256']==expected,'Current165 source '+name)
 npz=q['poses'];require(all(npz[k]==proof['pose_file'][k] for k in ('path','bytes','sha256')),'Exact already-produced172 pose artifact')
 p=Path(npz['path']);st=p.stat();require(st.st_size==npz['bytes'],'Pose producer bytes');a={}
 with p.open('rb') as f:
  R['NPZ_open_count']=1;R['NPZ_integrity_passes']=1;s=hashlib.file_digest(f,'sha256').hexdigest();require(s==npz['sha256'],'Pose producer SHA');f.seek(0)
  with np.load(f,allow_pickle=False) as packet:
   require(set(MEMBERS)<=set(packet.files),'Existing arrays are sufficient; do not create extra neural rollout')
   for name in MEMBERS:a[name]=packet[name];R['array_content_reads'][name]=1
 require(p.stat().st_mtime_ns==st.st_mtime_ns,'Readonly pose artifact changed')
 R['inputs'].append(dict(key='172_poses',path=str(p),bytes=st.st_size,sha256=s,opens=1,integrity_passes=1,NPZ_load_calls=1,only_members=list(MEMBERS)))
 n=len(a['actual_position']);nb,nh=a['blue_soft_done'].shape;require(0<n<=2000 and 0<nb<=80 and nh==32 and n==proof['actual_controls'] and nb==proof['blue_branch_count'],'Bounded actual172 dimensions')
 require(a['actual_position'].shape==(n,31,3) and a['actual_rotation'].shape==(n,31,3,3) and a['actual_public_age'].shape==(n,),'Actual pose dimensions')
 require(a['blue_position'].shape==(nb,32,31,3) and a['blue_rotation'].shape==(nb,32,31,3,3) and a['blue_survival'].shape==a['blue_valid'].shape==(nb,32),'Blue pose dimensions')
 require(a['branch_input_public_age'].shape==(nb,) and a['origin'].shape==(3,),'Age/origin dimensions');require(all(np.isfinite(v).all() for v in a.values()),'Finite existing poses')
